Additional Restrictions: Vehicle Restriction No Access For Emergency Starting 5/22/2023 7:00 AM\n\nDetails: ST. MARY PARISH, La. - The Louisiana Department of Transportation and Development (LADOTD) announces that beginning Monday, May 22, work will begin on a $25,969,337.50 rehabilitation project on the La. 182 bridge over the Atchafalaya River in St. Mary Parish. The project will begin at the Berwick side and end at the Morgan City side of the bridge. Work will consist of cleaning, painting, bridge repair, and related work. Work is expected to take place Monday through Friday from 7 a.m. to 5 p.m., weather permitting. Nighttime and weekend work is also expected. The bridge will be closed to traffic beginning May 23. The detour route will consist of U.S. 90. Emergency vehicles will not have access to the area. DOTD appreciates your patience and reminds you to please drive with caution through the construction site and be on the lookout for work crews and their equipment.\n\nSeverity: None\n\nLanes closed: All\n\nLane impact: All lanes closed\n\nReported/start: 5/22/2023, 7:00:00 AM\n\nSchedule: <b>Mon, Tue, Wed, Thu, Fri:</b><br/>7:00 AM - 5:00 PM<br/><br/>\n\nSchedule details: [object Object]\n\nReporting agency: ERS\n\nSource: 511LA"

All Lanes Intermittent. Width Restriction: 10ft Starting 10/16/2023 7:00 AM\n\nDetails: ST. MARTIN PARISH, La. - The Louisiana Department of Transportation and Development (DOTD) announces that beginning Monday, October 16, construction will begin on a $11.96 million project along La. 347 from La. 350 to La. 351. This project consist of milling asphaltic concrete, pavement patching, asphalt overlay, drainage improvements and related work. The work is estimated to take place Mondays through Fridays from 7 a.m. to 5 p.m., weather permitting. Intermittent lane closures will be required to perform the work, therefore, drivers should expect delays. Emergency vehicles will have access through this area. The road will be open to regular traffic with a 10-foot lane restriction on oversized loads passing through the work zone. The project is estimated to be complete in February 2025. DOTD appreciates your patience and reminds you to please drive with caution through the construction site and be on the lookout for work crews and their equipment.\n\nSeverity: None\n\nLane impact: All Lanes Intermittent\n\nReported/start: 10/16/2023, 7:00:00 AM\n\nSchedule: <b>Mon, Tue, Wed, Thu, Fri, Sat, Sun:</b><br/>7:00 AM - 5:00 PM<br/><br/>\n\nSchedule details: [object Object]\n\nReporting agency: ERS\n\nSource: 511LA"

Icon: 0,0,0,2,1,1,"CONSTRUCTION / ROADWORK\n\nEvent type: pavingoperations\n\nRoad: LA-10\n\nDirection: Both Directions\n\nDescription: Paving Operations on LA-10 Both Directions from Lebeau Housing Rd MM (16) to Plant Rd MM (16). Lane Intermittent. Starting 2/3/2025 7:00 AM\n\nDetails: ST. LANDRY PARISH, LA - The Louisiana Department of Transportation and Development (DOTD) announces that beginning Monday, February 3, 2025, construction will begin on a $5.03 million project to resurface and improve drainage structures on a 4.7-mile stretch of La. 10 from Little Wauksha Bridge to U.S. 71 in St. Landry Parish. The project will include borrow work, cross drain pipe installation, precast concrete box culverts, asphalt removal, milling asphalt pavement, pavement patching, asphalt concrete overlay, and related work. Intermittent lane closures will be required to perform the work; therefore, drivers should expect delays. Work is estimated to take place Monday through Friday from 7 a.m. until 5 p.m., weather permitting. There will also be separate planned full road closure events during the project which are necessary to install drainage structures across the roadway. The Department will publish press releases for these planned road closure events, and detour routes will be posted. Emergency vehicles will have access to this area, except during planned full road closure events. This project is estimated for completion in December 2025. Width Restriction: 12ft Starting 6/10/2025 4:00 AM\n\nDetails: Traffic Shift Scheduled for LA 70 in Ascension, St. James Parish Ascension Parish, La.- The Louisiana Department of Transportation and Development (DOTD) announces a new traffic configuration on LA 70 between LA 22 and the Sunshine Bridge, scheduled to begin at 4 a.m. on Tuesday, June 10, 2025. Traffic will shift into a split-lane configuration to accommodate ongoing construction. Westbound traffic will use the outside lane of the existing LA 70 alignment, while eastbound traffic will use the outside lane of the newly constructed LA 70 alignment. This change will require motorists to travel on both the old and new portions of LA 70. Driveway and roadway access along the corridor may be modified, and drivers should remain alert. In addition, the traffic signal at the intersection of LA 70 and LA 3125 will be removed, and the intersection will be converted to a permanent roundabout. Motorists are urged to proceed with caution through the new configuration. The lane shift is necessary to allow the contractor to complete median work and pave the final layer of asphalt on the inside lanes of LA 70. During this phase, oversized or permitted loads exceeding 12 feet in width will not be allowed through the work zone. Drivers should contact the DOTD Permit Office to obtain alternate routing. Project update: Following the traffic shift, construction crews will move into the final phase of the project. Remaining tasks include milling and overlaying the westbound lanes of LA 70 from the Ascension-St. James Parish line to the Sunshine Bridge, as well as paving the final wearing course on the eastbound lanes between the Sunshine Bridge and LA 22. Additional work includes forming and pouring the center island and red concrete truck apron at the LA 70/LA 3125 roundabout, installing concrete islands at LA 70 and Raymond Tullier Road, and paving associated turnouts, turn lanes, crossovers, and driveways. Width Restriction: 12ft Starting 9/18/2025 7:00 AM\n\nDetails: IBERIA PARISH, La. - Today, the Louisiana Department of Transportation and Development (DOTD) announced that construction has started on a $50 million dollar I-49 South (Geaux South) project in Iberia Parish to further upgrade U.S. 90 to interstate standards. The project will involve constructing two overpasses on U.S. 90 eastbound and westbound over the Patout Railroad between La. 85 (Patout Road) and La. 668. PROJECT SCOPE Work associated with the construction of the overpasses will consist of precast concrete piles, precast-prestressed concrete girders, mechanically stabilized earth wall concrete panels, temporary detour roads, subgrade treatment, class II base course, drainage structures, concrete pavement, signage, striping, and related work. WORK SCHEDULE Work is expected to take place Monday through Saturday from 7:00 a.m. to 5:00 p.m. along with intermittent night and Sunday work, weather permitting. TRAFFIC IMPACTS U.S. 90 westbound is currently reduced to one lane with a 12-foot wide maximum lane restriction. This lane reduction and width restriction will continue to remain in effect. U.S. 90 eastbound will experience intermittent lane closures with a 12-foot wide maximum lane restriction that is currently in place. This restriction will continue to remain in effect. U.S. 90 eastbound and westbound will remain open throughout this project. Emergency vehicle will have access to this area but may encounter delays. For more information on this project, please visit the project page. WHAT IS GEAUX SOUTH? The Geaux South program is a multi-billion, multi-year construction initiative to convert approximately 160 miles of the current U.S. 90 corridor from Lafayette to New Orleans to Interstate 49. SAFETY REMINDER DOTD appreciates your patience and reminds you to please drive with caution through the construction site and be on the lookout for work crews and their equipment.
